- **replica-diff**: Compares an app clone against the original: a feature parity score from the feature matrix (weighted by must, should, could) with the missing list in build order, plus a screenshot layout diff that ignores colour so a rebrand does not count against you. Two standard-library Python tools. Use when the user says "how close is my clone", "compare it to the original", "what's missing", "parity check", "diff the screens", "is it ready", or after /replica-test. - **replica-diff**: Fase 7 de replica — mide paridad funcional (parity.py) y visual (imgdiff.py) entre el original y el clon: contraste de features por severidad, diff de layout por regiones y evidencia por hallazgo. Úsala para saber si un clon ya iguala a la app original, cerrar un gate de paridad o documentar diferencias aceptadas. - **replit-the-local-filesystem-is-ephemer-e55d55b59cbf71f1**: Replit: the local filesystem is ephemeral, persist with the database. The rule: anything that must survive a restart does not belong on local disk. - Small app data -&gt; Replit's built-in key/value database (db["user_data"] = data) - Files -&gt; Object Storage (storage.upload_from_text("user_data.json", ...)) - Relational data -&gt; the provisioned PostgreSQL database The agent will happily write a JSON file to disk and call it persistence.
